## Caption & Legend Auditor

### efigure1.png

---
TITLE:              Missing — the caption opens with a descriptive phrase ("Kernels at the same design resolution…") but there is no concise **bold** title sentence as required.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) linear scale near the main lobe, (b) dB relative to the peak; both described in order and labelled lowercase in-figure.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — the figure shows deterministic smoothing/leakage kernels, so no error bars are expected. (Caption should state explicitly that curves are analytic/deterministic kernels, not estimates from data, so a reader does not look for dispersion.)

SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — no realization count is needed for deterministic kernels, but the caption must say so. It also does not state whether the dB curves in (b) were computed analytically or evaluated on a discrete frequency grid (and, if the latter, the grid density/zero-padding factor used, since the visible ripple structure depends on it).

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — equivalent degrees of freedom ν are given for every curve (good, and this satisfies the journal's "state the estimator's degrees of freedom where noise levels are compared" rule), but (i) ν is never defined as *equivalent/effective degrees of freedom* of the spectral estimator, (ii) the convention used to compute ν is not named, and (iii) no statistical test is involved, which should be implicit but is currently ambiguous because ν values invite a variance comparison with no stated basis.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: ν (equivalent degrees of freedom); N (series/window length in samples — value given but quantity unnamed); Δ (sampling interval); R (design resolution / half-power bandwidth); L (number of Slepian tapers); 𝒦(f) (the kernel itself — never named); "half-power width"; "skirts"; "recipe (b′)". dB is acceptable as standard.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — all six curves are mapped to colors/linestyles in the caption, and panel (a) carries an in-figure legend. Advisory: panel (b) has no in-figure legend, so it relies entirely on the caption mapping; this is acceptable but worth a one-clause reminder ("colors as in (a)").

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the caption depends on "Table 2" for the design parameters and on "recipe (b′)" from the main text; neither is intelligible from the figure alone. Definitions of ν, R, Δ, N, L and 𝒦 are also external.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold title sentence leading the legend (journal style: brief descriptive caption with a title-like opening; IEEE prohibits in-figure titles, so the legend must carry it).
  - [BLOCKING] Error-bar/dispersion status not declared. Even though none are present, the legend must state that the curves are deterministic kernels (no realizations, no error bars) — otherwise the reader cannot tell whether the ripples in the black and red traces are estimation noise or genuine kernel structure.
  - [BLOCKING] ν is used six times but never defined; as the quantity the journal requires for noise-level comparison, its definition and computation convention must appear in the legend.
  - [BLOCKING] External dependency on "Table 2" and "recipe (b′)" breaks self-containment. The design parameters (N = 256, R = 8/(NΔ), taper family, taper fraction, parabola half-power width) must be stated in the legend itself.
  - [ADVISORY] Symbol collision: "recipe (b′)" is visually confusable with panel label (b). Rename to avoid ambiguity in the same sentence as panel (b).
  - [ADVISORY] Axis quantities unexplained: fNΔ is a normalized (dimensionless) frequency and 𝒦(f)/NΔ a normalized kernel amplitude; neither normalization is stated.
  - [ADVISORY] "the skirts differ by 40 dB" is unquantified — specify at which |fNΔ| the 40 dB spread is measured, and between which two curves.
  - [ADVISORY] The dotted vertical lines in (b) are not described; the caption mentions "the ideal box (dotted)" only in the (a) context.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 2089 px across a 181 mm double-column width ≈ 293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI floor for color line art. Supply at 600 DPI (or vector EPS/PDF) for line art.
  - [ADVISORY] Verify tick-label and legend text are ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at 181 mm final width; the six-entry legend in (a) is the item most at risk after downscaling.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Recast the legend along these lines:
    "**Six spectral-estimation kernels matched to a common design resolution.** Deterministic smoothing/leakage kernels 𝒦(f) (no data, no realizations, hence no error bars), computed for a record of N = 256 samples with sampling interval Δ and design half-power resolution R = 8/(NΔ). Frequency is shown as the dimensionless product fNΔ; in (a) the kernel is normalized by NΔ and plotted on a linear scale near the main lobe, and in (b) the same kernels are plotted in decibels relative to their peak. Curves: ideal rectangular ("box") kernel of width R (black dotted, also marked by vertical dotted lines in (b)); Slepian multitaper kernel with L = 7 tapers (black, ν = 14.0); periodogram after a 25 % split-cosine-bell taper, then smoothed by a parabolic kernel of the same half-power width (red, ν = 16.7); and untapered (green, ν = 17.2), Hann-tapered (cyan, ν = 8.9) and Bohman-tapered (olive dashed, ν = 7.3) periodograms, each then smoothed by the box. Colors and linestyles in (b) are as in (a). Here ν denotes the equivalent degrees of freedom of the resulting spectral estimator, ν = 2(∫𝒦)²/∫𝒦², which sets its variance; larger ν means a less noisy but not necessarily better-localized estimate. All main lobes agree in half-power width to within X %, whereas at |fNΔ| = 20 the sidelobe (skirt) levels span ≈ 40 dB, from ≈ −33 dB (untapered, green) to ≈ −95 dB (Bohman, olive)."
  - Fill in the actual matched-main-lobe tolerance (X %) and confirm the quoted skirt levels against the plotted curves.
  - State the frequency grid / zero-padding factor used to render (b), since the ripple envelope of the multitaper and untapered curves is grid-dependent.
  - Replace "recipe (b′)" with a non-colliding label (e.g., "method C" or the explicit description already given) and move the Table 2 cross-reference to the main text, keeping the numeric parameters in the legend.
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS, or raster at 600 DPI for the 181 mm double-column width; keep RGB and confirm no in-figure title was introduced.

### efigure2.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — "Dropping the leakiest taper." is present as the first sentence but is not set in bold (and is a slogan rather than a descriptive title of what is plotted).

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) and (b) described in order and keyed to lowercase labels matching the figure.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — the figure shows a single realization, so no error bars/shaded bands are required; however, the variability visible in each trace is the estimator's own sampling fluctuation and is currently characterized only via ν, which is incomplete (see below).

SAMPLE SIZE:        Partially stated — N = 1024 samples and "one realization" are given, but the number of realizations behind the figure (n = 1) should be stated explicitly and unambiguously for both panels, and it must be stated that panel (b) is the same single realization (not an average over realizations).

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is performed (none needed), but per journal policy on noise-level comparisons: (i) ν is given for the periodogram, box-smoothed, L = 7, L = 6, and parabola-smoothed estimates but is MISSING for the adaptively weighted L = 7 estimate (olive); (ii) ν is never defined as "equivalent degrees of freedom"; (iii) the quoted floor offsets in (b) ("20 dB high", "10 dB high", "halves that") are given without tolerance or the frequency band over which they were measured.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density), AR(4) (fourth-order autoregressive process — coefficients or a citation needed), ν (equivalent degrees of freedom), L (number of tapers), Δ (sampling interval), R (defined numerically as 8/(NΔ) but not named, e.g., "smoothing-window half-power bandwidth"), dB reference level, and the time–bandwidth product NW (or W) of the Slepian/DPSS tapers is never given at all.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legend in (a) plus colors restated in the caption. Panel (b) has no legend; the caption should state that (b) uses the same color coding as (a) and that the periodogram and true PSD are omitted from (b).

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader cannot reproduce or fully interpret the figure without: the AR(4) parameters (or citation), the taper time–bandwidth product NW for the multitaper estimates, the definition of the "box" and "parabola" smoothing windows, the definition of ν, the sampling interval Δ, the ν of the adaptive estimate, and an explanation of the horizontal zero reference line in (b).

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Equivalent degrees of freedom are quoted for five of the six estimators but omitted for the adaptively weighted multitaper estimate; the journal requires the estimator's degrees of freedom wherever noise levels are compared, and (b) is explicitly a noise-floor comparison. Give the (frequency-dependent or band-averaged) ν for the adaptive estimate.
  - [BLOCKING] The number of realizations behind the displayed traces is not stated unambiguously for panel (b). State "single realization (n = 1); no averaging across realizations" for both panels, or give n if any averaging was used.
  - [BLOCKING] Multitaper estimates are shown without the time–bandwidth product NW (or bandwidth W) and without stating that Slepian/DPSS tapers were used. The entire point of the figure ("dropping the leakiest taper", L = 7 vs. L = 6) is uninterpretable without NW.
  - [BLOCKING] ν is used five times and never defined; PSD and AR(4) are undefined on first use in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Figure title is not bold and is non-descriptive; it states a conclusion rather than the content.
  - [ADVISORY] The second half of the (b) sentence is an interpretive claim ("floors 20 dB high ... track the truth") with no stated measurement band or tolerance; quantitative claims in a caption should specify where and how they were measured.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) color key not restated; also unstated that the periodogram (grey) and true PSD are absent from (b).
  - [ADVISORY] The black horizontal line at 0 dB in (b) is unexplained (zero-bias reference).
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 2089 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈292 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum for color line art and well below the preferred 600 DPI. Supply vector EPS/PDF or re-render at ≥600 DPI.
  - [ADVISORY] Dense grey periodogram and thin olive/cyan traces overlap heavily in (a); at 88–181 mm final size some traces may be unresolvable, and the caption cannot compensate for this.
  - [ADVISORY] The dB reference (dB relative to what, e.g., process variance per unit frequency) is not stated on either axis or in the caption.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite the opening as a bold, descriptive title, e.g.: **Effect of discarding the highest-order (leakiest) Slepian taper on multitaper spectral bias.**
  - Insert the missing experimental parameters in the first sentence: "Single realization (n = 1) of a fourth-order autoregressive, AR(4), process [ref/coefficients in Table X], N = 1024 samples, sampling interval Δ, analyzed with Slepian (DPSS) tapers of time–bandwidth product NW = ___ (W = ___/(NΔ)); smoothing-window half-power bandwidth R = 8/(NΔ)."
  - Define ν explicitly once: "ν denotes the equivalent (chi-square) degrees of freedom of each estimator."
  - Add the adaptive estimator's degrees of freedom: "adaptively weighted estimate with L = 7 (olive, band-averaged ν = ___, range ___–___ across frequency)."
  - Add a colour-key sentence for (b): "(b) Same realization and same colour coding as (a); the periodogram and the true PSD are omitted. The black horizontal line marks zero bias."
  - Qualify the quantitative claims: "Averaged over fΔ ∈ [0.35, 0.5], the bias floor is +___ ± ___ dB (untapered/box), +___ ± ___ dB (L = 7), +___ ± ___ dB (L = 6), and +___ ± ___ dB (adaptive and cosine-taper/parabola)."
  - Spell out PSD on first use and state the dB reference level on the axis or in the caption.
  - Resubmit as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, ≤10 MB, with all text ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at 181 mm final width; verify the in-figure legend text and axis labels after rescaling.

### efigure3.png

---
TITLE:              Missing (no bold lead-in title; the first sentence is descriptive but not formatted as a bold title)
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b), (c) described in order; advisory that the lower-right quadrant contains the shared legend and is not a panel (d), which should be stated explicitly
ERROR BARS:         Not present — correctly and explicitly justified ("exact kernel and degrees-of-freedom computations, with no sampling variability")
SAMPLE SIZE:        Partial — N = 256 samples is given, but the swept parameter values (α range, L values, number of Welch segments/windows, smoother half-widths) are not stated
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable (deterministic computation) — the estimator degrees of freedom requirement is satisfied by panel (a); no hypothesis test is performed, and the caption should say so in one clause so a reader does not look for one
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: N (record length in samples), Δ (sampling interval), α (time–bandwidth product of the Slepian sequences), L (number of tapers), "box"/"parabola"/"Gaussian" (post-periodogram smoothing kernels), dB reference level in (c); ν is defined (equivalent degrees of freedom) — good
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present (in-figure legend) — but caption method names do not match legend strings verbatim
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — undefined symbols (N, Δ, α, L), no definition of what "then box/parabola/Gaussian" means operationally, the dB reference for (c) is unstated, and the dotted line is explained only by cross-reference to Table 2
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold title as the first sentence of the caption; IEEE style for this submission expects a brief bold descriptive lead-in and no title inside the figure (the figure itself correctly has no internal titles).
  - [BLOCKING] Core symbols used on the axes and in the legend (N, Δ, α, L) are never defined in the caption. A reader cannot interpret the abscissa "half-power width × NΔ" or the legend entries "L = 2α − 1" and "L = 2α − 2" without the main text.
  - [BLOCKING] The dotted vertical reference line is explained only by cross-reference ("the 8/(NΔ) width of Table 2"). State what the line represents self-containedly (e.g., "the common resolution at which the methods are compared in Table 2").
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) is in dB but the reference level is not given (peak relative to the kernel's peak response at zero frequency? relative to unit gain?). State it.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) ordinate is logarithmic over eight decades and panel (c) is in dB; state the axis scaling in the caption for readers scanning the figure only.
  - [ADVISORY] Method names in the prose do not match the in-figure legend strings ("cosine-tapered periodogram followed by a parabola" vs. "25% cosine taper, then parabola"; "Welch's method with 50% overlap" vs. "Welch, Hann, 50% overlap"). Mismatched naming forces the reader to map nine curves by inference.
  - [ADVISORY] The number of Welch segments (and hence why that curve extends to half-power width ≈ 24.6/(NΔ) while others stop near 16) is not stated; this asymmetric x-extent will read as a plotting error without explanation.
  - [ADVISORY] The empty lower-right quadrant holding the legend may be misread as a missing panel (d); state "the shared legend applies to all panels" and consider labelling it as a legend box rather than leaving the quadrant unannotated.
  - [ADVISORY] "no sampling variability" is good practice but should be paired with an explicit statement that no error bars or confidence intervals are shown because the quantities are analytic, so a reviewer does not flag their absence.
  - [ADVISORY] Marker shapes (circle/square/triangle/diamond) are partially redundant with colour, which is good for greyscale, but the black solid, black dashed and black dotted curves plus the dark-grey triangles are close in tone; verify distinguishability at 88 mm/181 mm final width and ≥6 pt.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Recast the opening as a bold title plus a self-contained setup, e.g.: "**Variance, spectral leakage and side-lobe level versus achieved resolution.** All quantities are exact analytic kernel and degrees-of-freedom computations for a record of N = 256 samples with sampling interval Δ; no data were simulated, so there is no sampling variability and no error bars or confidence intervals are shown."
  - Add symbol definitions: "α is the time–bandwidth product of the Slepian (DPSS) sequences and L the number of tapers averaged; 'then box/parabola/Gaussian' denotes a single-taper periodogram subsequently smoothed with a boxcar, parabolic (Welch–Bartlett–Priestley), or Gaussian frequency-domain kernel."
  - State the sweep: "For each method the bandwidth parameter (α = …–…, smoother half-width = …–… bins, Welch segment length giving M = … 50 %-overlapped Hann segments) was swept; each marker is one parameter value."
  - Replace the Table 2 cross-reference with a self-contained statement: "The vertical dotted line marks a half-power width of 8/(NΔ), the common resolution used for the equal-resolution comparison."
  - Give the dB reference in (c): "(c) Peak kernel magnitude beyond 1.5× the half-power half-width, in dB relative to the kernel peak."
  - Make caption method names verbatim identical to the legend entries, listed in legend order.
  - Add "Panel labels are lowercase (a)–(c); the boxed legend at lower right applies to all three panels."
  - Export as vector EPS/PDF (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) at 181 mm double-column width, RGB, and confirm all tick labels, axis labels and legend text render at 8–10 pt (no element below 6 pt) at that final size.

TITLE:              Missing — caption opens with a cross-reference ("Fig. 5 in full"), not a bold descriptive title
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Incomplete — rows (a–d), (e–h), (i–l), (m–p) are described collectively, but the four *columns* are never named in this caption (deferred to Fig. 5); no per-panel description for (a), (b), (c), (d) individually
ERROR BARS:         Not present — deterministic/analytic curves; acceptable, but should be stated explicitly that no stochastic averaging is involved
SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — number of tapers/filters K per column (visibly 7, 12, 11, 7 in (i)–(l)) and the record length N (and Δ) are not given
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is performed (fine), but the journal requires the estimator's degrees of freedom where noise/variance is compared; the equivalent DOF (≈2·Σc_i²-based or 2K) implied by the eigen-weight ladders in (i)–(l) is not reported. Also missing: definition of the resolution parameter R, bandwidth–time product NW, and the numerical basis (grid size, FFT length) for the kernels in (m)–(p)
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: Q (the matrix whose eigen-tapers are shown), R (resolution bandwidth), N, Δ, f, f′, k, n, i, h_{k−n}, h_k^{(i)}, c_i, 𝒦(f), "sliding sinc", "Thomson's estimate", "Slepian sequences" (acceptable if defined earlier, but not defined here), dB reference ("relative to peak" is stated — good)
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partially present — in-figure keys exist in (b) (f′NΔ = 0–3) and (d)/(e) (i = 1–4); however panel (a) shows 7 unlabeled colored curves, panel (c) reuses the (b) color code with no key, and panels (f)–(h) rely on the (e) key without cross-reference. The dotted vertical lines in (m)–(p) are defined; the dotted *horizontal* gridlines are not. Line color in (m)–(p) is unexplained (single trace or two overlapping traces?)
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the caption defers the entire column definition to Fig. 5, so a reader cannot identify which estimator each column represents; N, Δ, R, K, and Q are undefined
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold descriptive title as the first sentence; caption begins as a cross-reference fragment ("Fig. 5 in full").
  - [BLOCKING] Columns are not identified in this caption ("Columns as in Fig. 5"). Each of the four columns must be named here (e.g., sliding sinc / Welch–Bartlett / Hann-tapered / Thomson multitaper) so the figure stands alone.
  - [BLOCKING] Estimator degrees of freedom are not stated for any column, although the figure explicitly compares weight ladders and kernel leakage — required by journal policy when noise/variance behaviour is compared. State K and the equivalent DOF (e.g., DOF = 2(Σc_i)²/Σc_i²) per column.
  - [BLOCKING] Key parameters undefined: N, Δ, R, Q, and the number of filters/tapers per column. Without N the abscissa k ∈ [0, 256] and the axis label fNΔ are uninterpretable.

  - [ADVISORY] The quoted half-power widths "5.84, 8.03, 8.03 and 7.41/(NΔ)" read ambiguously; the 1/(NΔ) unit must be attached to all four values.
  - [ADVISORY] Out-of-band power percentages (0.05%, 0.13%, 1.39%, 0.22%) are given without stating the integration limits convention or the numerical method/grid used.
  - [ADVISORY] Statement "for Thomson's estimate, whose weights are equal, the Slepians are one of many orthonormal bases of the span" is a text-level argument; keep but add that panels (d) and (h) are therefore identical by construction (they appear identical in the image) — otherwise readers will suspect a duplicated panel.
  - [ADVISORY] At 2091 px across a 181 mm double-column width the effective resolution is ≈293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum (600 DPI preferred for line art). Re-export as vector EPS/PDF.
  - [ADVISORY] Axis-label and tick fonts should be verified to be ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) after scaling to 181 mm; the 16-panel grid puts them at risk.
  - [ADVISORY] y-axis limits differ between (d)/(f)/(g)/(h) (0.2 vs 0.25 top); harmonize to permit visual comparison across the row, or note the difference.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite the opening as a bold standalone title, e.g.: "**Filter-bank construction, eigen-tapers, eigen-weights, and spectral windows for four multitaper-family estimators.**"
  - Name the columns explicitly: "Columns (left to right): <estimator 1>, <estimator 2>, <estimator 3>, and Thomson's multitaper estimate (same ordering as Fig. 5)."
  - Add a parameter sentence: "All panels use N = 256 samples, sampling interval Δ, and resolution bandwidth R = <value>/(NΔ); the bank contains K = 7, 12, 11, and 7 filters for the four columns, respectively, giving equivalent degrees of freedom of <…>, <…>, <…>, and <…>."
  - Describe panels individually: "(a) filter bank h_{k−n} as constructed; (b) modulated filters h_k cos(2πf′kΔ) for f′NΔ = 0–3 (key inset); (c) the same filters normalized to their maxima, colors as in (b); (d)–(h) …".
  - Add the shared-key statement: "In (d)–(h), black, blue, green, and red denote eigen-tapers i = 1–4 (key inset in (d) and (e); same colors in (f)–(h))."
  - Define Q: "Q is the <…> matrix whose eigenvectors are the eigen-tapers; c_i are its eigenvalues (eigen-weights), plotted normalized in (i)–(l)."
  - For (m)–(p): "𝒦(f) is the composite spectral window in dB relative to its peak; vertical dotted lines mark |f| = R/2, horizontal dotted lines are gridlines at 20 dB intervals; curves are single traces computed on a <M>-point frequency grid."
  - Append units correctly: "Half-power widths are 5.84, 8.03, 8.03, and 7.41 (in units of 1/(NΔ)); 0.05%, 0.13%, 1.39%, and 0.22% of the total window power lies outside |f| ≤ R."
  - State that no stochastic averaging is involved: "All curves are analytic/deterministic; no error bars apply."
  - Confirm and note that (d) and (h) are identical by construction, or remove the duplicate and re-letter.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b), (c) described in order and panel labels in the image are lowercase parenthetical, consistent with journal style.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — the figure shows deterministic single-epoch estimates and a difference curve; no error bars or distributions are plotted. This should be stated explicitly so a reader does not assume the curves are means.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Partially stated — record length (N = 1024 samples, 8 s, 128 Hz, one epoch, one channel) is given, but the **number of sliding windows averaged in the magenta trace is not stated**, and the number of epochs/subjects (n = 1) is only implicit.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable (no hypothesis test) — but the journal-mandated estimator degrees of freedom **is** given (ν = 17) for the multitaper estimate only; the equivalent degrees of freedom of the box-smoothed periodogram and of the sliding-sinc average are not given, and these are the quantities being compared.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (electroencephalogram/-graphy); Δ (sampling interval) in R = 8/(NΔ); α (half-bandwidth / time–bandwidth parameter, relation to R = 2α/(NΔ) not stated); N_s (defined by usage only, never named as "sliding-window length"); "Slepian" tapers (DPSS) acceptable but consider one clarifying word. PSD, λ_i, ν are defined.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present in-figure (panels a, b, c all carry legends) and restated in the caption; however the in-figure label "Box − multitaper" does not match the caption wording "smoothed periodogram", and the (c) legend entry "Maximum |difference|" does not state that it refers to the sliding-sinc trace only.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the definition of ν is deferred to Section~\ref{sec:periodogram}; the sliding-window construction is under-specified (a window of length N_s = 16N cannot be slid inside a record of length N without zero-padding or periodic extension, which is not explained); the number of windows averaged is absent; α is undefined.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold lead-in title sentence. Journal/house style requires a brief bold descriptive title as the first sentence of the legend.
  - [BLOCKING] Number of windows/realizations behind the averaged magenta trace in (a)–(c) is not stated, and the mechanism for sliding a length-16N window over a length-N record (zero padding? circular extension? padded to 16N total?) is unexplained. As written, panel (c) cannot be reproduced.
  - [BLOCKING] Equivalent degrees of freedom are given only for the multitaper estimate. Because the panel compares three estimators' noise/bias behaviour, ν must be given (or stated as identical by construction) for all three.
  - [BLOCKING] Caption relies on an external cross-reference (Section~\ref{sec:periodogram}) for the definition of ν, defeating the self-containment requirement.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) x-axis is the dimensionless ratio N_s/N, but the caption says "against the window length N_s". Mismatch between axis and caption.
  - [ADVISORY] EEG, α, and Δ are used without definition in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Terminology drift between in-figure legends and caption ("Box" vs "untapered periodogram smoothed with a box"; "Sliding sinc" vs "sliding-window average"; "Maximum |difference|" without stating which pair).
  - [ADVISORY] Data provenance ("the authors' archive") gives no ethics/consent statement, channel name, or preprocessing (filtering, detrending, mean removal), all of which affect the PSD shown.
  - [ADVISORY] Raster resolution: 1829 px across a 181 mm double-column width ≈ 255 DPI, below the ≥300 DPI (≥600 preferred) requirement for line art. Supply EPS/PDF vector or re-render ≥600 DPI.
  - [ADVISORY] Axis/legend fonts are serif (Times); acceptable to IEEE but sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica) is preferred — check that the smallest text is ≥6 pt at 181 mm reproduction.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) reports agreement to "3×10⁻¹³ dB", but the green trace floor sits nearer 10⁻¹⁴–10⁻¹³ dB; state whether the quoted number is the maximum or the median absolute difference (same for the 0.06 dB figure).

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add a bold title, e.g. **"Three equivalent routes to a resolution-matched PSD estimate."**
  - Rewrite the sliding-window description to be reproducible: give the padding/extension scheme, the hop size (one sample), and the exact count of windows averaged (e.g. "M = … windows").
  - State ν for all three estimators, or write "all three estimators have ν = 17 equivalent degrees of freedom by construction," and define ν inline (e.g. "ν = 2 × (number of independent spectral averages)") rather than by section reference.
  - Define EEG, α (and its link to R via R = 2α/(NΔ)), Δ, and N_s inside the caption.
  - Change "against the window length N_s" to "against the window-length ratio N_s/N".
  - Harmonise the in-figure legend strings with the caption wording, and relabel the (c) magenta entry "Sliding sinc − multitaper, maximum |difference|".
  - Add "No error bars are shown; all traces are deterministic estimates from a single 8 s epoch (n = 1 channel, 1 epoch)."
  - Add one clause on preprocessing and a data-ethics/consent phrase, or cite the dataset.
  - Re-export as vector EPS/PDF (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, <10 MB, and confirm minimum font size ≥6 pt at final width.

### figure2.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — the opening sentence ("Every estimator is the true spectrum blurred by a kernel, plus noise.") reads as an interpretive claim rather than a bold descriptive title; IEEE TBME expects a brief descriptive caption with no title *inside* the figure, but the journal/house style you specified requires the first sentence to function as a concise bold title.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete in order for (a)–(d); (e)–(h) treated as a group — Partial. The grouped sentence does supply per-panel numbers (half-power widths, leakage percentages) in the same 1–4 order, but panels (e), (f), (g), (h) are never individually introduced ("(e) kernel of the periodogram (Fejér kernel), (f) …"). A reader must infer the one-to-one mapping (a)→(e), (b)→(f), etc., which is stated nowhere.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — correctly so, since a single realization is plotted. This is explicitly declared ("One realization (N=1024)"), which satisfies the realization-count requirement. No error-bar definition is needed, but see ISSUES about the absence of any variability indicator for a noise-level comparison.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated — N = 1024 samples, one realization. Number of frequency bins / whether the periodogram is zero-padded is not stated (advisory).

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial —
  - Equivalent degrees of freedom ν = 2, 2, 16.7, 98.4 are given and mapped to (a)–(d): fully satisfies the journal's "state the estimator's degrees of freedom where noise levels are compared."
  - Absent: the definition/convention used for ν (e.g., ν = 2/∑ of squared smoothing weights, or Walden's equivalent-dof formula) and whether the taper is included in the ν = 2 for panel (b).
  - Absent: no statistical hypothesis test is performed, so test statistic and p-values are Not applicable — this should be evident but is fine as is.
  - Absent: the AR process is not specified (order, coefficients, innovation variance, or a citation), so ν and the "true PSD" cannot be reproduced.
  - Absent: the definition of "half-power width" for the smoothing parabola vs. for the kernel is used in both senses without distinguishing them.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density — never expanded); Δ (sampling interval, used in every axis label f Δ and in every width expression); AR is avoided but "autoregressive process" lacks an order; dB re. peak is stated only for (e)–(h); 𝒦(f) (the y-axis symbol in (e)–(h)) is never tied in words to "expected-value kernel"; Ŝ(f) (y-axis of (a)–(d)) is never named; R is defined in situ (acceptable); ν is defined in situ (acceptable); "Fejér kernel" is invoked without identifying it as the periodogram kernel of panel (e).

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial —
  - Blue dashed = True PSD is stated in the caption and an in-figure legend appears in (a) only; the legend is not repeated in (b)–(d), so the caption must carry it — it does for blue/dashed but never states that the **black solid** trace is the estimate.
  - The dark green trace in (e)–(h) is never identified in the caption (single trace, so low risk, but it should be named).
  - The black rectangle + dashed leader lines marking the zoom region are described only as "zoomed-in view of the main lobe is shown on the top left"; the box/leader convention is not explained, and the inset axis limits differ in (h) (0.36–0.44) from (e)–(g) (0.39–0.41), which is not flagged.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — missing (i) the AR process definition needed to reproduce the true PSD, (ii) expansion of PSD and definition of Δ, (iii) identification of the black solid curve, (iv) per-panel introduction of (e)–(h) and the explicit (a)↔(e) pairing, (v) definition of "expected-value kernel" (i.e., E{Ŝ(f)} = ∫𝒦(f−f′)S(f′)df′), (vi) the reason the kernel is centered at fΔ = 0.4, (vii) note that the inset frequency span in (h) differs.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold descriptive title as the first sentence of the legend. The present opener is a thesis statement, not an identification of what is plotted.
  - [BLOCKING] Panels (e)–(h) are not described individually and their correspondence to (a)–(d) is never stated; a reader cannot be certain that (e) is the periodogram kernel rather than, say, the tapered kernel.
  - [BLOCKING] The black solid curve in (a)–(d) is not defined in the caption; the in-figure legend exists only in panel (a), so panels (b)–(d) have no key at all.
  - [BLOCKING] PSD is used without expansion and Δ — which appears in every axis label and every quantitative statement — is never defined. Without Δ the widths "7/(NΔ)", "30/(N�ODΔ)", "8/(NΔ)" etc. are uninterpretable.
  - [BLOCKING] The generating process is unspecified (AR order, coefficients, innovation variance, or citation). The caption asserts a "true PSD" and derived ν values that cannot be checked or reproduced.
  - [ADVISORY] The convention behind ν is not given (which equivalent-dof formula; whether tapering is folded into ν = 2 for panel (b)). Since the figure's entire point is a variance/bias trade-off, the estimator's dof convention should be explicit per journal policy.
  - [ADVISORY] "our measure of broadband leakage" uses first person and defines a metric inside a results caption; state it as a definition ("broadband leakage, defined here as the fraction of kernel power outside |f| ≤ 8/(NΔ)").
  - [ADVISORY] The inset in (h) spans 0.36–0.44 while (e)–(g) span 0.39–0.41; unequal zoom windows invite misreading of relative main-lobe width and are not mentioned.
  - [ADVISORY] 𝒦(f) and Ŝ(f) axis symbols are never named in words in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Number of frequency points / zero-padding factor not stated; this affects the apparent roughness of (a)–(b) and the visual density of the Fejér sidelobes in (e)–(f).
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 2090 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum for color line art (600 DPI preferred). Re-export at ≥600 DPI, or submit vector EPS/PDF.
  - [ADVISORY] Supply final artwork as TIFF/EPS/PDF for production; PNG is acceptable for review only. Confirm RGB color mode and 8–10 pt sans-serif labels at final size (the inset tick labels are the smallest text and must not fall below 6 pt after scaling).
  - [ADVISORY] Panel letters are lowercase in parentheses, which matches the requested style — keep this; ensure the caption uses the same lowercase form throughout (it does).

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add a bold title as the first sentence, then keep the thesis as the second sentence. Example: "**Every spectral estimator is the true spectrum blurred by a kernel, plus noise.** One realization (N = 1024 samples, sampling interval Δ) of the fourth-order autoregressive process of Eq. (12) …"
  - Expand PSD at first use: "power spectral density (PSD)". Define Δ as the sampling interval and note that frequency is displayed as the normalized quantity fΔ ∈ [0, 0.5].
  - Specify the process fully or cite it: order, coefficients, and innovation variance, plus the statement that the true PSD spans 65 dB and has two half-power-width-7/(NΔ) peaks separated by 30/(NΔ).
  - State the color/line key explicitly and note that it applies to all of (a)–(d): "In (a)–(d), black solid = estimate Ŝ(f) from the single realization; blue dashed = true PSD. The legend in (a) applies to (b)–(d)."
  - Introduce (e)–(h) panel by panel and state the pairing: "(e)–(h) Expected-value kernel 𝒦(f) (dark green) of the estimator in the panel directly above — (e) periodogram (Fejér kernel), (f) 25% cosine taper, (g) taper + parabolic smoothing with R = 8/(NΔ), (h) taper + parabolic smoothing with R = 48/(NΔ) — plotted in dB relative to its own peak and centered at fΔ = 0.4."
  - Define the kernel relation once: "𝒦 is defined by E{Ŝ(f)} = ∫ 𝒦(f − f′) S(f′) df′, normalized to unit area."
  - Give the dof convention and confirm the taper's effect: "Equivalent degrees of freedom ν, computed as [formula/citation], are ν = 2, 2, 16.7 and 98.4 for (a)–(d) respectively; tapering leaves ν = 2 because no averaging is performed."
  - Recast the leakage metric as an explicit definition rather than a first-person aside, and pair each number with its panel letter: "Half-power widths are 0.8, 1.2, 8.2 and 47.8/(NΔ) for (e)–(h); the fraction of kernel power outside |f| ≤ 8/(NΔ) (broadband leakage) is 1.3%, 0.02%, 0.12% and 65%."
  - Explain the inset convention and the differing span: "Insets (top left of each panel) magnify the main lobe over the frequency interval marked by the black rectangle; note the wider interval (0.36–0.44) in (h)."
  - Add a one-clause statement that a single realization is shown so that no error bars or variability intervals are plotted, and that estimator variance is instead summarized by ν.
  - State the number of frequency points and any zero-padding used for the periodogram and kernel evaluations.
  - Re-export at ≥600 DPI or as vector EPS/PDF, verify all text ≥6 pt at final printed size, and confirm the file remains under 10 MB.

### figure3.png

---
TITLE:              Present but **not bold** — "Choosing the resolution." is a suitable short title; it must be set in bold (`\textbf{Choosing the resolution.}`) as the first sentence of the legend.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) and (b) both described, in order, with lowercase labels matching the figure.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — and correctly declared ("the curves are ensemble averages and carry no error bars"). This satisfies the error-bar requirement.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated — N = 1024 samples per record; 300 independent realizations for panel (b). Good. (Add n for the peak-region curve if it is the same 300 realizations — state explicitly that all four curves use the same 300 realizations.)

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is performed (acceptable for a bias–variance figure), but the journal requires the **estimator's equivalent degrees of freedom** wherever noise levels are compared. Panel (b) plots a noise (standard-deviation) curve against bias across smoothing widths, so the EDoF of the smoothed periodogram (as a function of R, or of RNΔ) must be given. Also missing: how the "noise" and "bias" curves were computed from the 300 realizations (ensemble SD vs. mean deviation), and whether the RMS error is computed on the dB scale before or after averaging over frequency.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density); AR(4) (fourth-order autoregressive process — and its coefficients/innovation variance or a pointer to the defining equation); Δ (sampling interval); f (frequency, and whether fΔ is cycles per sample); RMS (spelled out as "root-mean-square" in text, but expand on first use in the legend since it also appears on the ordinate as "RMS error"); dB. Units of x_k in panel (a) are also unstated (arbitrary units?).

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legend for (b); (a) keyed in the caption (black = record, dashed = taper, red = tapered record). Circled markers and dotted vertical lines are also explained. Minor: state explicitly that the circles in (b) mark three specific RNΔ values and give those numerical values.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — (i) the abscissa is labelled *RNΔ* but the caption discusses behaviour "with R" only, never defining the product RNΔ or why the resolution is normalized this way; (ii) the circled points are defined only by reference to another figure's panels, so their R values cannot be read without leaving this figure; (iii) PSD/AR(4)/Δ undefined; (iv) EDoF of the estimator not given.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Equivalent degrees of freedom of the smoothed-periodogram estimator are not stated, although panel (b) explicitly compares noise (estimator standard deviation) against bias across smoothing widths. Required by journal policy.
  - [BLOCKING] Axis/caption mismatch: the ordinate quantity is plotted against *RNΔ* while the caption speaks only of *R*. Define RNΔ (dimensionless smoothing bandwidth = half-power width × record length in normalized frequency) so the abscissa is interpretable.
  - [BLOCKING] Title not bold.
  - [ADVISORY] The circled points are identified only as "panels (b), (c) and (d) of Fig. \ref{fig:motif}", which (1) collides notationally with panel (b) of this figure and (2) forces the reader to another figure. Give the numerical RNΔ values.
  - [ADVISORY] Undefined abbreviations/symbols: PSD, AR(4), Δ, f, RMS, dB; units of x_k.
  - [ADVISORY] Estimator definition incomplete: "noise" and "bias" are not defined operationally (ensemble SD across the 300 realizations vs. mean(estimate) − true PSD), nor is it stated that RMS error is averaged over frequency in dB.
  - [ADVISORY] The peak-region band 0.09 ≤ fΔ ≤ 0.16 is given, but the whole-band range (presumably 0 ≤ fΔ ≤ 0.5) is not.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 992 × 1258 px gives ≈286 DPI at 88 mm single-column width and only ≈139 DPI at 181 mm double-column width — below the ≥300 DPI (600 DPI preferred) requirement for coloured line art. Supply vector EPS/PDF for production.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) has no legend inside the figure; if space allows, a small in-figure key would reduce caption load. Also confirm all axis-label and tick fonts are ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at final printed width.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Bold the title and restructure as: **Choosing the resolution.** followed by panel descriptions.
  - Insert an explicit definition of the abscissa and of the estimator's degrees of freedom, e.g. "The abscissa RNΔ is the parabolic kernel's half-power width R expressed in units of the Rayleigh resolution 1/(NΔ), where N = 1024 is the record length and Δ the sampling interval; the corresponding equivalent degrees of freedom of the smoothed estimate are ν ≈ … (e.g. ν ≈ c·RNΔ, c = …), rising from ν = 2 at RNΔ = 0 to ν ≈ … at RNΔ = 64."
  - Define operationally: "Bias is mean{10 log10 Ŝ(f)} − 10 log10 S(f) and noise is the standard deviation of 10 log10 Ŝ(f), both computed pointwise across the same 300 realizations and then root-mean-squared over frequency; no error bars are shown because the curves are ensemble summaries (n = 300 realizations for all four curves)."
  - Expand abbreviations on first use: "power spectral density (PSD)", "fourth-order autoregressive, AR(4), process (coefficients given in Section …; innovation variance σ² = …)", "sampling interval Δ", "root-mean-square (RMS)", "decibels (dB, 10 log10 scale)". State units of x_k or label them arbitrary units.
  - Replace the cross-figure pointer with numbers: "Circled markers indicate RNΔ = 0, 8 and 48, the three resolutions illustrated in Fig. \ref{fig:motif}(b)–(d)."
  - State both scoring bands: "whole band, 0 ≤ fΔ ≤ 0.5 (solid), and peak region, 0.09 ≤ fΔ ≤ 0.16 (dashed)."
  - Give the dotted-line values numerically: "dotted vertical lines mark RNΔ = 7 (the half-power width of a single spectral peak) and RNΔ = 30 (the peak separation)."
  - Re-export as vector EPS/PDF (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) in RGB at the intended column width; keep file <10 MB.

### figure4.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — "Why the multitaper kernel is a box" is present as the first sentence but is not set in bold, and it is rhetorical rather than a declarative descriptive title.
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a)–(d) described in order, lowercase labels matching the figure.
ERROR BARS:         Not present — figure is deterministic/analytical; no error bars required, but see ADVISORY on degrees of freedom.
SAMPLE SIZE:        Not applicable as biological n; simulation parameters partially stated (N = 256, R = 8/(NΔ)). Missing: α (time–bandwidth product), Δ, and the number of tapers N used for the "all N tapers" curve in (c).
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable (no inference performed) — however, the journal's requirement to state the estimator's degrees of freedom (≈2L for L tapers) is unmet where L is varied in (c).
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: Δ (sampling interval), α (time–bandwidth product NWΔ or equivalent), R (resolution/analysis bandwidth), λ_i (eigenvalue / spectral concentration), 𝒦(f) (aggregate multitaper kernel), H^(i)(f) (DFT of the i-th taper), L (number of tapers), "Box" (ideal boxcar kernel), "Fejér's kernel" (used but not defined), i (taper index).
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present for (a), (b), (c), (d) via in-figure legends; caption duplicates (a). Missing: meaning of the vertical dotted lines in (b) is given (±R/2) but the identical vertical dotted lines in (c) are never identified; the open-circle markers in (d) are not described.
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — normalizations in the caption do not match the axis labels, several symbols are undefined, and the caption relies on an external equation reference \eqref{eq:identity}.
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Axis-label/caption mismatch, panel (b): the ordinate is labelled |H^(i)(f)|²/NΔ² but the caption calls it |H^(i)(f)|²/Δ. One of the two is wrong; a reader cannot reconcile the units.
  - [BLOCKING] Axis-label/caption mismatch, panel (c): the ordinate is labelled 𝒦(f)/NΔ but the caption defines the plotted quantity as Σ_{i≤L} λ_i|H^(i)(f)|²/(2αΔ). Either state that 𝒦(f) ≡ Σ_{i≤L} λ_i|H^(i)(f)|²/(2α) and that the plot is normalized by NΔ, or correct the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Core symbols are undefined in the caption: α, Δ, R, λ_i, 𝒦(f), H^(i)(f), L. Since α sets both the band R and the "usual L = 2α−1 = 7", its numerical value (α = 4) must be stated explicitly.
  - [BLOCKING] Caption title is not bold and is phrased as a question/claim rather than a descriptive title, contrary to the required "brief descriptive caption" convention.
  - [BLOCKING] The caption depends on an internal cross-reference, \eqref{eq:identity}, to convey the main point of (c); the figure is therefore not interpretable without the main text. Restate the identity in words or give it in closed form in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Vertical dotted lines at fNΔ = ±4 in panel (c) are not identified in the caption (only in (b)); state once that dotted vertical lines mark the band edges ±R/2 in both (b) and (c).
  - [ADVISORY] Open circles in (d) are not described (computed eigenvalues at integer taper index i, connected line for visual guidance only).
  - [ADVISORY] Degrees of freedom are not reported: for each L in panel (c) state the nominal equivalent degrees of freedom (≈2L, and 2·Σλ_i for the weighted estimator), as required when noise/variance levels implied by different L are compared.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) shows only tapers i = 1, 2, 7 but the caption says "seventh (red)" without noting why 7 was chosen (it is the L = 2α−1 boundary taper); one clause would make the figure logic clear.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) ordinate is clipped at ±0.20 and the h^(7) curve is truncated at the frame; state that the vertical axis is clipped, or expand the range.
  - [ADVISORY] The legend in (c) lists L = 9 (magenta) but that curve is visually indistinguishable from L = N (black) at this scale; note this in the caption so the reader does not search for a missing trace.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 2101 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈295 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum for colour line art (600 DPI preferred). Supply vector EPS/PDF for final submission.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (d) y-axis spans 10⁻¹⁵ to 10⁰ but data occupy only 10⁻¹⁰ to 10⁰; the empty decades waste space and reduce effective font size.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Recast the opening as a bold declarative title, e.g. **"Aggregation of Slepian tapers reproduces an ideal band-limited kernel."**
  - Harmonize all plotted quantities with the axis labels; define 𝒦(f) explicitly in the caption and state the normalizing factor once.
  - Add a parameter sentence: "Parameters: N = 256 samples, sampling interval Δ, time–bandwidth product α = 4, analysis bandwidth R = 2α/(NΔ) = 8/(NΔ); L denotes the number of tapers retained."
  - Add a symbol/statistics sentence: "h_k^(i) is the i-th discrete prolate spheroidal (Slepian) sequence, H^(i)(f) its DFT, and λ_i its in-band energy concentration; the estimator built from L tapers has ≈2L equivalent degrees of freedom (2L = 2, 6, 10, 14, 18 for L = 1, 3, 5, 7, 9)."
  - Describe every line style once: "Dotted vertical lines in (b) and (c) mark the band edges f = ±R/2; the dashed trace in (c) is the ideal boxcar of width R; in (d) the dotted and red dashed vertical lines mark i = 2α−1 = 7 and i = 2α = 8, and open circles are computed eigenvalues (line drawn for guidance)."
  - Replace the \eqref{eq:identity} dependency with a self-contained statement of the identity, e.g. "the complete taper set satisfies Σ_{i=1}^{N} λ_i|H^(i)(f)|² = 2α · (box of width R ∗ Fejér kernel)."
  - Note the clipped ordinate in (a) and the overlap of the L = 9 and L = N curves in (c).
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) at 181 mm width, confirm all tick and axis labels are ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at final size, and tighten the (d) ordinate range to 10⁻¹⁰–10⁰.

TITLE:              Not bold — an informative opening sentence exists ("Three ways to build a bank of tapers from one window, and the reference bank…") but it is not set as a bold lead-in title, and it is a fragment rather than a sentence.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars, shaded bands, or distributions appear in any panel; the panels are deterministic constructions. This is acceptable, but the caption should state explicitly that the curves/bars are analytic (noise-free) constructions, not estimates from data, so a reader does not look for missing uncertainty.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing (in the journal's sense) — N=256, L=7, and the segment/taper counts are given, but the caption never states that no realizations/windows are averaged (i.e., that these are deterministic and n is not applicable). STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is performed, so test statistic and p-values are not applicable. The estimator degrees of freedom ν is reported for all four banks, which satisfies the TBME requirement, but: (i) ν values are not paired to panel labels; (ii) the number of significant figures/rounding is inconsistent (13.4, 16.7, 17.2, 14.0 — fine, but state whether these are exact or rounded); (iii) the equation reference \eqref{eq:ladder} points outside the figure (the formula is fortunately reproduced inline, so this is advisory).

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: Δ (sampling interval), f′ (frequency-shift variable in the (b) legend, f′NΔ), λ_i (eigenvalues used as weights in (c)), c_i (called "eigen-weight" but its origin/definition is not given), i (taper/eigen-component index in (d) and (e)–(h)), "max h" (normalizing constant in the (c) ordinate), n (shift index in h_{k−n}), k (sample index), R (given a value but not named — resolution/half-power bandwidth), L (given a value but not named as the number of tapers), "eigen-taper," "ladder." "Fig. S4" (Supplementary Figure S4) should be spelled out once.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial/Missing — in-figure legends exist for (b) (f′NΔ = 0–3) and (d) (i = 1–4), but (a) plots seven colored copies and (c) four colored positions with no key; the caption does not say what the colors encode (e.g., "colors denote successive 50 %-overlap segment positions, left to right" and "colors denote sinc-window centre positions n = …"). The uniform navy bars in (e)–(h) need no key, but the caption should say the bars are ordered by decreasing weight/index i.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader cannot reconstruct the figure without the main text. Missing: definitions of Δ, f′, λ_i, c_i, i, R, L; the mapping of (e)–(h) onto (a)–(d) and onto the four ν values; the color keys for (a) and (c); the axis-normalization meaning in (c); and why the number of bars differs between panels (7 vs 12).

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Panels (e)–(h) are not described individually or in order; the four ν values are given as an unlabeled list, so the reader must guess which ladder belongs to which bank. TBME requires panel-by-panel description with lowercase labels.
  - [BLOCKING] Multiple symbols appearing on the axes and in the in-figure legends are undefined in the caption: Δ, f′, λ_i, c_i, i, "max h", n, k. A caption that leaves axis quantities undefined fails the self-containment test.
  - [BLOCKING] No statement of the number of realizations/windows behind each panel. Even though these are deterministic constructions, TBME's statistical-reporting rule requires the caption to state this explicitly (e.g., "analytic taper sequences; no averaging over realizations") and to give the total number of tapers/segments contributing to each ladder in (e)–(h).
  - [ADVISORY] The lead sentence is not a bold, standalone title; it is also a sentence fragment.
  - [ADVISORY] Colors in (a) and (c) carry information (segment position, window position) but have no in-figure legend and no caption key.
  - [ADVISORY] The caption relies on an external equation reference (\eqref{eq:ladder}) and on Supplementary Fig. S4; the inline formula mitigates the former, but "Fig. S4" should be expanded to "Supplementary Fig. S4."
  - [ADVISORY] The degrees of freedom ν are compared across four estimators without stating the rounding convention or whether they are theoretical (from the c_i ladder) or empirical.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) claims equivalences ("exactly the untapered periodogram smoothed with a box," "exactly Thomson's estimate with all N tapers weighted by λ_i") that are asymptotic; the qualifier "in the limit of a long window" applies to the first but its scope over the second is ambiguous.
  - [ADVISORY] Non-caption note: at 2090 px across a 181 mm double-column width the raster resolution is ≈293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum for color line art (600 DPI preferred). Supply the figure as vector EPS/PDF or re-render at ≥600 DPI.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Recast the opening as a bold title sentence, e.g.: "**Three constructions of a taper bank from a single window, and the reference multitaper bank.**"
  - Describe every panel in order and pair each ladder with its parent bank and ν, e.g.: "(e)–(h) Normalized eigen-weight ladders c_i/Σc_i for the banks in (a)–(d), respectively: (e) ν = 13.4 (7 segments), (f) ν = 16.7 (12 components), (g) ν = 17.2 (12 components), (h) ν = 14.0 (L = 7 tapers)."
  - Add a symbol glossary to the caption: "k, sample index; n, shift index; Δ, sampling interval; N = 256, record length; R = 8/(NΔ), half-power (resolution) bandwidth; f′, frequency shift (legend in (b) gives f′NΔ); i, taper/eigen-component index; λ_i, Slepian eigenvalues; c_i, weight of the i-th eigen-taper; max h, peak of the parent window used to normalize (c)."
  - Add explicit color keys: "(a) Colors denote the seven successive Hann segments (50 % overlap), ordered left to right. (c) Colors denote four successive one-sample shifts of the sinc window." Alternatively add in-figure legends to match (b) and (d).
  - State the statistical/estimator basis once: "All curves and weights are analytic constructions for N = 256; no averaging over data realizations is involved (n not applicable). Degrees of freedom are the theoretical values ν = 2(Σ_i c_i)²/Σ_i c_i², rounded to one decimal."
  - Expand "Fig. S4" to "Supplementary Fig. S4" and reproduce the ladder equation inline without relying on \eqref (or add "(Eq. 12 in the text)").
  - Tighten the panel (c) statement to scope the limit clearly: "…as the window length grows, this bank converges to the untapered periodogram smoothed with a boxcar, equivalently to Thomson's estimate using all N Slepian tapers with weights λ_i."
  - Supply the figure as vector EPS/PDF (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) at 181 mm width, and confirm all axis/legend text is ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at final size.

### figure6.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — the legend opens with a descriptive clause ("The estimators used in practice, applied to the record of Fig. X…"), not a short bold title sentence.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete for (a)–(d) and (e)–(h) in order, but the insets in (e)–(h) are never described (what is magnified, and why the box is at fΔ ≈ 0.4).

ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars or distributions are plotted; each panel shows a single realization and a deterministic kernel, so no error-bar definition is required. However, the 300-realization summary statistics quoted in the text of the legend carry no dispersion measure.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated — "one realization" for (a)–(d), N = 1024 samples, and "300 realizations" for the ensemble median absolute errors. Good. Missing: the number of frequency bins/half the DFT length used in computing the "median over the band," and the definition of that band.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — equivalent degrees of freedom ν are given for all four estimators (satisfies the journal's DoF requirement), but (i) ν is never identified in words as the equivalent/effective degrees of freedom, nor is the formula or reference for it given; (ii) no dispersion (IQR, quantiles, or SD) accompanies the 300-realization median absolute errors; (iii) the frequency band over which the median absolute error is computed is unspecified; (iv) no test is performed, so no test statistic/p-value applies — state explicitly that the comparison is descriptive, not inferential.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density); Slepian/DPSS lineage of the multitaper "seventh taper" (acceptable if defined earlier, but "Slepian taper" should be tied to the DPSS abbreviation used in the text); ν; R; Δ; N; L; Ŝ(f); 𝒦(f); "Fejér tails" (kernel name used without stating it is the Fejér kernel of the rectangular data window); "half-power width"; "skirt"; "flat top." Symbols on the axes (Ŝ(f), 𝒦(f), fΔ) must be defined in the legend even if defined in the text.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial/Missing — the in-figure key appears only in panel (a) ("Estimate," "True PSD"). Panels (b), (c), (d) use black, red and magenta solid curves with no key; the legend says only "true PSD blue, dashed" and never states that the solid curve in each of (b)–(d) is that panel's estimate, nor that the colour change is cosmetic (i.e., colour does not encode an additional variable). The dark-green single colour in (e)–(h) is also unexplained.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the legend depends on Fig. (motif) for both the data record and the kernel plotting convention, and on Table (ensemble) for the ensemble errors; the insets, axis quantities (Ŝ, 𝒦, fΔ), ν, R and the error band are not defined within the legend.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold, concise title sentence at the head of the legend.
  - [BLOCKING] Colour/line key incomplete: solid curves in (b), (c), (d) are unidentified in both the figure and the legend. A reader cannot confirm that black/red/magenta are the respective estimates rather than a third quantity.
  - [BLOCKING] Axis quantities and symbols undefined in the legend: Ŝ(f), 𝒦(f), fΔ (normalized frequency), Δ (sampling interval), N, R, L, ν. PSD is used unexpanded.
  - [BLOCKING] Insets in (e)–(h) are undescribed: the magnified frequency span (0.39–0.41), the vertical scale (dB), and the purpose (pass-band flatness/ripple) must be stated.
  - [BLOCKING] Statistical reporting incomplete under the journal's rule: ν is given numerically but never named as the equivalent degrees of freedom, and the 300-realization medians are reported without any dispersion measure or the frequency band over which the median is taken.
  - [ADVISORY] "The four half-power widths are 8.2, 7.2, 8.2 and 7.8/(NΔ)" — units attach only to the last value; write each as a multiple of 1/(NΔ) or express all relative to R.
  - [ADVISORY] "This realization has more leakage than is typical" is a subjective judgement; justify it quantitatively (e.g., percentile of the 300-realization error distribution) or remove it, since a deliberately atypical realization in a single-realization figure invites reviewer objection.
  - [ADVISORY] Kernel out-of-band power values (1.4%, 0.22%, 0.12%, 0.48%) need the integration convention stated (two-sided power fraction outside |f| ≤ R, normalized how?).
  - [ADVISORY] Legend is long and mixes description with interpretation/discussion (e.g., "levels off 20 dB above the true spectrum," "side lobes are spaced at the scale of the peaks"); IEEE prefers interpretation in the body text.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) y-axis label is clipped/tight ("Ŝ(f) (dB)" sits at the left edge); verify the label is not cropped at 88/181 mm final width and that all tick labels are ≥ 6 pt.
  - [ADVISORY] Raster resolution: 2090 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈ 293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum and well below the 600 DPI preference for line art. Submit as vector (EPS/PDF) or re-render at ≥ 600 DPI.
  - [ADVISORY] Only panel (a) carries the in-figure legend; consider a single shared legend placed outside the axes so it does not overlap the (a) curve near the peak.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Recast the opening as a bold title plus a self-contained setup, e.g.: "**Four practical spectrum estimators and their smoothing kernels.** Single realization (n = 1 realization, N = 1024 samples, sampling interval Δ) of the process of Fig. X; the true power spectral density (PSD) is the blue dashed curve in (a)–(d), and the solid coloured curve in each of (a)–(d) is that panel's estimate Ŝ(f) (colour distinguishes panels only and encodes no additional variable). Abscissa is normalized frequency fΔ; ordinate is power in dB. Resolution half-bandwidth R = 8/(NΔ); ν denotes the equivalent (chi-square) degrees of freedom of each estimator."
  - Add the missing key explicitly: "(a) green, (b) black, (c) red, (d) magenta solid = estimate; blue dashed = true PSD (all four panels)."
  - Describe the insets once: "(e)–(h) Smoothing kernels 𝒦(f) in dB, centred at fΔ = 0.4; insets magnify the boxed region 0.39 ≤ fΔ ≤ 0.41 on a −50 to +5 dB scale to show pass-band flatness and ripple."
  - Complete the statistics sentence: "Median absolute error of Ŝ(f) over the band |f|Δ ∈ [a, b] (M = … frequency bins) is 11.6, 4.6, 0.9 and 0.8 dB for this realization, and 5.3, 2.3, 1.0 and 1.0 dB (median [IQR]: …) across 300 independent realizations (Table Y). Equivalent degrees of freedom: ν = 17.2, 14.0, 16.7 and 17.6, computed as [formula/reference]. No hypothesis test is performed; the comparison is descriptive."
  - Expand every abbreviation and symbol at first use in the legend: PSD, DPSS/Slepian, Fejér kernel, half-power (−3 dB) width, side-lobe skirt.
  - Move interpretive statements ("inherits the Fejér tails," "side lobes are spaced at the scale of the peaks") to the main text, or compress them into one short sentence at the end of the legend.
  - Express all half-power widths in consistent units: "half-power widths 8.2, 7.2, 8.2 and 7.8 in units of 1/(NΔ), i.e. 1.03R, 0.90R, 1.03R and 0.98R."
  - Re-export as vector EPS/PDF (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, with all text in a sans-serif face at 8–10 pt at 181 mm final width; confirm the panel (a) y-label is not clipped and keep file under 10 MB.
  - Keep panel labels lowercase (a)–(h) as drawn — this already matches IEEE style — and confirm no in-figure titles are added.

TITLE:              Not bold — opening sentence is an interpretive claim ("The estimators used in practice agree to within their own noise."), not a bold, concise descriptive title

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) and (b) described in order, matching the lowercase in-figure labels

ERROR BARS:         Not present — but summary distribution statistics (median, 95th percentile) are reported without stating the number of frequency bins they were computed over

SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — no epoch length, sampling rate, number of frequency bins, subject/record identification, or number of averaged segments for the smoothed estimate

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — equivalent degrees of freedom (ν = 14, 16.7, 17.6) are given for all three estimators (good, and required by this journal); missing: (i) the number of samples/bins N behind the median and 95th-percentile statistics, (ii) any formal test or confidence interval for the claim that the medians are "smaller than the estimators' own noise" (no test statistic, no p-value), (iii) definition/derivation of the constant 4.3 in 4.3√(2/ν), (iv) numerical value of R and of the smoothing bandwidth used in recipe (b′)

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (spell out at least once); ν (state explicitly "equivalent degrees of freedom, ν"); "recipe (b′)" (an internal manuscript label with no meaning to a reader of the figure alone); Ŝ(f) (y-axis quantity — state it is the one-sided power spectral density estimate and the dB reference); "smoothed"/"averaged" in (b) are used as shorthand for recipe (b′) and Welch without being tied to those labels

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — black/red/magenta mapped to the three estimators both in-figure and in the caption; consistent between panels

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — reader cannot reconstruct the analysis without the main text: recipe (b′), the smoothing half-power width R, the data record (length, fs, channel), and the origin of the 4.3 factor are all external

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold descriptive title as the first sentence; the current lead sentence is a conclusion rather than a title.
  - [BLOCKING] The number of realizations/windows/bins behind the reported distribution statistics (median absolute difference, 95th percentile) is not stated. This journal explicitly requires the count behind any error bar or distribution.
  - [BLOCKING] Sample size / data provenance absent: epoch duration, sampling rate, number of frequency bins, and record identity are not given anywhere in the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined abbreviation/label "recipe (b′)" and undefined symbol ν; the figure legend itself prints "Recipe (b′)", so a reader has no way to know what estimator this is.
  - [ADVISORY] Noise-level comparison is asserted but not tested: state it as a comparison of point estimates against the asymptotic standard deviation, or supply a confidence interval / exact p-value. Also justify 4.3 dB (= 10/ln10 × …) so the degrees-of-freedom argument is verifiable.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 991 px wide corresponds to ≈286 DPI at 88 mm single-column width, just under the 300 DPI minimum, and only ≈139 DPI if placed at 181 mm. Regenerate as vector (EPS/PDF) or ≥600 DPI raster.
  - [ADVISORY] "smoothed" and "averaged" in the panel (b) text are not the terms used in the in-figure legend ("Recipe (b′)", "Welch, 9 Hann segments"); align the wording.
  - [ADVISORY] Half-power widths (0.91, 1.03, 0.97 Hz) are listed without stating which value belongs to which estimator explicitly; the reader must infer the ordering.
  - [ADVISORY] Axis quantity Ŝ(f) in dB has no stated reference level (dB re µV²/Hz?), so absolute ordinate values are uninterpretable.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add a bold title and restate the claim later, e.g.: "**Fig. 7. Practical spectral estimators of an EEG epoch agree to within their own estimation noise.**"
  - Insert data provenance and bin count: "Single 13.5-s EEG epoch (channel …, fs = … Hz, N = … samples) from the record of Fig. 1; all statistics in (b) are over the M = … frequency bins in 0–40 Hz."
  - Define every label and symbol in the caption: "…electroencephalogram (EEG)…"; "ν, equivalent degrees of freedom"; "recipe (b′): cosine-tapered periodogram smoothed by a parabolic kernel of half-power width R = … Hz (Section III-B)".
  - Pair each half-power width with its estimator: "half-power widths 0.91 Hz (multitaper), 1.03 Hz (recipe (b′)) and 0.97 Hz (Welch)".
  - Make the noise comparison explicit and quantitative: "Expected standard deviation of the log-spectrum, 10/ln(10)·√(2/ν) = 4.3√(2/ν) dB, equals 1.6, 1.5 and 1.5 dB for ν = 14, 16.7 and 17.6; the observed median absolute differences (0.6 and 0.9 dB, M = … bins) lie below these values." Add a test with exact p-value if the comparison is to be treated inferentially.
  - State the dB reference for Ŝ(f) on the axis label or in the caption.

### figure9.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — "Sleep spindles at three resolutions." is concise and informative but is not set in bold in the LaTeX source (journal/house style expects a bold lead phrase; also confirm IEEE TBME style, which typically uses "Fig. 9." followed by roman text — if bolding is not permitted, state so and keep the lead sentence)

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b–g) grouped by window length with left/right method assignment, (h) all described in order

ERROR BARS:         Not present — panel (h) shows single point estimates (circles/squares) with a connecting segment; no dispersion is plotted. This should be stated explicitly ("markers are single point estimates; no error bars") so a reader does not read the connecting line as a range/interval.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — no statement of the number of subjects/recordings (appears to be n = 1 epoch from one subject), and no statement of the number of windows underlying each spectrogram (40 s with 0.1 s step ⇒ ≈391 windows for 1 s, ≈381 for 2 s, ≈361 for 4 s). TBME requires the number of realizations/windows behind any spectral estimate to be stated.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — equivalent degrees of freedom (ν) are given for every setting (satisfies the "state the estimator's df where noise levels are compared" rule), but the following are absent: (i) how ν was computed (analytic formula vs. empirical), (ii) the definition of "achieved half-power width" (half-power bandwidth of the effective smoothing kernel, measured how?), (iii) the number of windows/segments contributing to each estimate, (iv) sampling rate and NFFT/zero-padding, without which the resolution claims cannot be checked. No hypothesis test is performed, so test statistic/p-values are not applicable — say so explicitly ("no statistical tests were performed; all quantities are descriptive").

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: N2 (define as AASM stage N2 non-REM sleep); C4-M1 (state as 10–20 electrode C4 referenced to contralateral mastoid M1); PSD (power spectral density, in the colour-bar label); α (time–bandwidth product NW, used in "$L=2\alpha-1$" and "$\alpha=1$"); L (number of Slepian/DPSS tapers — implied but never named); ν (equivalent/effective degrees of freedom — used in panel (h) labels, only loosely tied to "twice its area"); R (used in the (h) row labels "R = 2 Hz", "R = 1 Hz" — never defined anywhere in the caption); "parabola" smoother (state as a parabolic/Epanechnikov spectral smoothing kernel and give its span in Hz or bins); "small-α regime" (annotation in (h) is not explained)

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present but incomplete — (a) line colours are identified in the in-figure legend; (h) markers are keyed both in-figure and in the caption. Missing: explicit statement that the shared colour bar is PSD in dB re 1 µV²/Hz with limits ≈ −6 to +13 dB, that it is common to (b)–(g), and that values are clipped/saturated at the limits (the dark-red low-frequency band is clearly saturated). The white rectangle is described but the reader is not told it is the same object in all six panels and that its width axis is time and height axis is frequency. Dotted vertical grid lines in (h) and the horizontal segment joining paired markers are unexplained.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader cannot reconstruct the analysis from figure + caption. Missing: recording/subject provenance and dataset, sampling rate, filter types/orders for the 0.3–35 Hz and 11–16 Hz bands, how the five spindles were identified (visual scoring? by whom? how many scorers?), taper type, smoother span, number of windows, definitions of α, R and ν, and colour-bar range/clipping.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Error-bar/dispersion status in (h) is never stated; per TBME rules the number of realizations/windows behind any plotted quantity must be given. Neither the marker semantics (point estimate, no dispersion) nor the window count is provided.
  - [BLOCKING] Sample size absent: number of subjects/epochs (apparently a single 40 s N2 epoch) and number of analysis windows per spectrogram are not stated.
  - [BLOCKING] The symbol R, which appears in every row label of panel (h), is never defined in the caption or figure — the panel is uninterpretable as drawn.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined abbreviations/symbols used in the figure: PSD, N2, C4-M1, α, L, ν, "small-α regime".
  - [ADVISORY] Figure title is not bold; also verify IEEE TBME permits a bold lead phrase (TBME captions are conventionally "Fig. 9. ..." in roman).
  - [ADVISORY] Colour-bar range, its common application to (b)–(g), and saturation/clipping of the high-power <4 Hz band are not stated; readers may mistake saturated regions for genuine plateaus.
  - [ADVISORY] Spectral-estimation details missing: taper family (DPSS/Slepian assumed), smoothing-kernel span in Hz, NFFT/zero-padding, detrending, and whether PSD is one-sided.
  - [ADVISORY] Spindle annotation provenance missing: "five spindles shaded" — scored by whom, using what criteria (AASM 11–16 Hz, ≥0.5 s)?
  - [ADVISORY] Statement "twice its area is approximately ν" is cryptic; area of a time × frequency box is dimensionless only after multiplication (s·Hz), and the relation to ν (=2·TW-type count) needs one clarifying clause.
  - [ADVISORY] Last sentence ("Short windows keep the spindles brief and blur them in frequency, and long windows do the opposite") is interpretation rather than description; acceptable but should follow, not replace, the missing methodological facts.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 1981 px across a 181 mm double-column width ≈ 277 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum for colour line art (600 DPI preferred). Re-export at ≥300 DPI (≥2140 px wide) and supply TIFF/EPS/PDF for production; PNG is acceptable for review only.
  - [ADVISORY] Check that all tick labels and the ν annotations in (h) are ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at 181 mm final width; the (h) annotations appear small relative to the axis labels.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Replace the caption with a version such as: "**Sleep spindles at three spectral resolutions.** (a) One 40 s epoch of AASM stage N2 non-REM sleep from a single subject (n = 1 recording; scalp electrode C4 referenced to contralateral mastoid M1), band-pass filtered 0.3–35 Hz (black; zero-phase 4th-order Butterworth) and 11–16 Hz (red, vertically offset by −100 µV); grey bands mark five spindles identified by visual scoring according to AASM criteria (11–16 Hz, ≥0.5 s) by one expert scorer. (b)–(g) Power spectral density (PSD) estimated in sliding windows of 1 s (b, c), 2 s (d, e) and 4 s (f, g), stepped by 0.1 s (391, 381 and 361 windows, respectively; sampling rate f_s = ___ Hz, NFFT = ___, linear detrending per window). Left column: multitaper estimate with L = 2α − 1 discrete prolate spheroidal (Slepian) tapers, L ≥ 1, where α = NW is the time–bandwidth product. Right column: single cosine-tapered periodogram smoothed across frequency with a parabolic (Epanechnikov) kernel of span ___ Hz. All six panels share the colour bar at right (PSD in dB re 1 µV²/Hz, clipped at −6 and +13 dB; values below/above are saturated). The white rectangle in each panel has width equal to the window length and height equal to the achieved half-power width, so it depicts that panel's time–frequency resolution cell; twice its area (s × Hz) approximates the equivalent degrees of freedom ν. (h) Achieved half-power width (abscissa) for each window length and target resolution R (the requested half-power width), for the multitaper estimate (black circles) and the smoothed periodogram (red squares); ν, the equivalent degrees of freedom of each estimator, is printed beside every marker. Markers are single point estimates from this one epoch; no error bars are shown and no statistical tests were performed. Horizontal segments merely join the two methods at a common setting and dotted vertical lines are grid references. At 1 s, α = 1 and the practical rule gives L = 1 ('small-α regime'), so the multitaper width is 1.1 Hz (ν = 2.0) versus 2.1 Hz (ν = 4.7) for smoothing; for α = 2 the two widths converge. Short windows preserve spindle onset/offset but blur frequency; long windows do the reverse."
  - Define R explicitly in the caption and, if space permits, expand the (h) row labels to "window 1 s, target R = 2 Hz" to make the panel readable stand-alone.
  - Add the window counts per spectrogram and the sampling rate; these are the quantities TBME requires for any spectral estimate.
  - State the colour-bar limits and that data are clipped; consider a gamma or percentile-based scaling, or a secondary annotation, so the saturated <4 Hz band does not dominate.
  - State in one clause that markers in (h) carry no dispersion, to pre-empt reviewer queries about error bars.
  - Re-export the figure at ≥300 DPI (600 DPI preferred) as TIFF/EPS/PDF, RGB, ≤10 MB, sized to 181 mm width; verify smallest font ≥6 pt at that size.
  - Confirm bolding of the lead sentence is allowed by TBME; if not, keep the lead phrase in roman and drop the bold markup.

## Statistical Integrity Checker

### efigure1.png

---

**Context note:** This figure plots deterministic, analytically/numerically evaluated spectral-estimator kernels (smoothing windows) as continuous functions of frequency. It contains no sampled measurements, no replicates, and no inferential comparisons, so most items in the statistical-visualization checklist are formally not applicable. The review below therefore focuses on the items that *do* transfer to deterministic function plots: axis integrity, curve discriminability, range truncation, and caption–figure consistency.

---
PLOT TYPE:          Appropriate — Overlaid line plots on (a) linear and (b) log/dB ordinates are the standard and correct presentation for kernel/window response functions. No bar, pie, or error-bar encoding is present, so none of the distribution-hiding concerns apply. The dual linear + dB panel pairing is good practice: it shows main-lobe shape and sidelobe/skirt decay, which no single scaling can convey.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Not applicable (but see advisory) — There is no statistical n. The analogous quantities are the design parameters $N=256$, $R=8/(N\Delta)$, $L=7$, and the equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu$ for each curve. All of these live only in the caption; none are inferable from the figure panels themselves. Since $\nu$ is the key figure of merit being traded off against skirt decay, it is worth surfacing it inside the plot.

COMPARISON:         Valid — All six kernels are plotted on identical axes at a matched design resolution, which is the correct "paired" framing for this comparison (the whole point is equal half-power width, differing skirts).
